import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

# Define the state and utility functions
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec, rho=1.0):
        vec = np.array(vec, dtype=float)
        norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
        self.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.rho = rho
    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic functions
def uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    if p.sum() != 0:
        p /= p.sum()
    return -np.sum(np.where(p>0, p*np.log(p), 0.0))

def simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal Dynamics
PHI = 1.61803398875
def oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(state.vec, 1)
    vec = state.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
    new.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec
    return new

def folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    vec = -np.abs(state.vec)
    norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
    new.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec
    new.rho *= PHI
    return new

def solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
        new.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec
    return new

# Run the simulation
initial_vec = [1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0]
state = DimensionalState(initial_vec)

records = []

for step in range(1, 11):
    state = oscillation(state) if step % 2 == 1 else folding(state)
    records.append({
        'step': step,
        'uncertainty': uncertainty(state),
        'simplicity': simplicity(state),
        'uniqueness': uniqueness(state),
        'continuity': continuity(state),
        'self_similarity': self_similarity(state),
        'interconnectivity': interconnectivity(state),
        'solidity': solidity(state),
        'paradox': paradox(state),
        'flux': flux(state)
    })

# Final smoothing step (Hexagram 9 resolution)
state = solution(state)
records.append({
    'step': 11,
    'uncertainty': uncertainty(state),
    'simplicity': simplicity(state),
    'uniqueness': uniqueness(state),
    'continuity': continuity(state),
    'self_similarity': self_similarity(state),
    'interconnectivity': interconnectivity(state),
    'solidity': solidity(state),
    'paradox': paradox(state),
    'flux': flux(state)
})

df = pd.DataFrame(records)

# Display the dataframe
import ace_tools as tools; tools.display_dataframe_to_user("UC Trajectories", df)

# Plot the trajectories
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
for col in df.columns[1:]:
    plt.plot(df['step'], df[col], label=col)
plt.title("Universal Characteristic Trajectories Over Steps")
plt.xlabel("Step")
plt.ylabel("Value")
plt.legend()
plt.show()

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# Define the state class
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec: np.ndarray, rho: float = 1.0):
        self.vec = vec / np.linalg.norm(vec) if np.linalg.norm(vec) != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.rho = rho
    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p = p / p.sum() if p.sum() != 0 else p
    return -np.sum(np.where(p > 0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Solution (neighbor averaging) dynamic
def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        smooth = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        new.vec = smooth / np.linalg.norm(smooth) if np.linalg.norm(smooth) != 0 else smooth
    return new

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from ace_tools import display_dataframe_to_user

# Constants
PHI = 1.61803398875

# State class
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec, order=0, freq=1.0, rho=1.0):
        norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
        self.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.order = order
        self.freq = freq
        self.rho = rho
    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.order, self.freq, self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    total = p.sum()
    if total != 0:
        p = p / total
    return -np.sum(np.where(p > 0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal dynamics
def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(state.vec, 1)
    v = state.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(v)
    new.vec = v / norm if norm != 0 else v
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    v = -np.abs(state.vec)
    new.vec = v / np.linalg.norm(v) if np.linalg.norm(v) != 0 else v
    new.rho *= PHI
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        v = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        new.vec = v / np.linalg.norm(v) if np.linalg.norm(v) != 0 else v
    return new

# Initialize
initial_vec = np.array([1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0])
state = DimensionalState(initial_vec)
records = []

# Run 10 steps: oscillation on even, folding on odd
for i in range(1, 11):
    if i % 2 == 1:
        state = apply_oscillation(state)
        mode = 'Oscillation'
    else:
        state = apply_folding(state)
        mode = 'Folding'
    # Compute UCs
    ucs = {
        'Uncertainty': calc_uncertainty(state),
        'Simplicity': calc_simplicity(state),
        'Uniqueness': calc_uniqueness(state),
        'Continuity': calc_continuity(state),
        'Self-Similarity': calc_self_similarity(state),
        'Interconnectivity': calc_interconnectivity(state),
        'Solidity': calc_solidity(state),
        'Paradox': calc_paradox(state),
        'Flux': calc_flux(state)
    }
    records.append({
        'Step': f"{i} ({mode})",
        'ρ (rho)': state.rho,
        **ucs
    })

# Final I Ching resolution: apply Solution
state = apply_solution(state)
ucs = {
    'Uncertainty': calc_uncertainty(state),
    'Simplicity': calc_simplicity(state),
    'Uniqueness': calc_uniqueness(state),
    'Continuity': calc_continuity(state),
    'Self-Similarity': calc_self_similarity(state),
    'Interconnectivity': calc_interconnectivity(state),
    'Solidity': calc_solidity(state),
    'Paradox': calc_paradox(state),
    'Flux': calc_flux(state)
}
records.append({
    'Step': 'Resolution (Solution)',
    'ρ (rho)': state.rho,
    **ucs
})

# Display results
df = pd.DataFrame(records)
display_dataframe_to_user("PCTM Loop Resolution Sequence", df)

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import math
from ace_tools import display_dataframe_to_user

# Constants
PHI = 1.61803398875

# State class and dynamics
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec, order=0, freq=1.0, rho=1.0):
        vec = np.array(vec)
        norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
        self.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.order = order
        self.freq = freq
        self.rho = rho

    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.order, self.freq, self.rho)

def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(state.vec, 1)
    combined = state.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(combined)
    new.vec = combined / norm if norm != 0 else combined
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    neg_abs = -np.abs(state.vec)
    new.vec = neg_abs / np.linalg.norm(neg_abs) if np.linalg.norm(neg_abs) != 0 else neg_abs
    new.rho *= PHI
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        avg = 0.5 * (fwd + bck)
        new.vec = avg / np.linalg.norm(avg) if np.linalg.norm(avg) != 0 else avg
    return new

# Universal Characteristic calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    total = p.sum()
    p = p/total if total != 0 else p
    return float(-np.sum(np.where(p > 0, p * np.log(p), 0)))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return float(math.log(state.rho + 1))

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return float(1 - np.max(state.vec**2))

def calc_continuity(state):
    return float(np.mean(state.vec))

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return float(np.std(state.vec))

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return float(np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1]))

def calc_solidity(state):
    return float(np.linalg.norm(state.vec))

def calc_paradox(state):
    return float(abs(1 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2))

def calc_flux(state):
    return float(np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec)))

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

# Constants and utility functions
PHI = 1.61803398875

class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec, rho=1.0):
        self.vec = np.array(vec, dtype=float)
        norm = np.linalg.norm(self.vec)
        if norm != 0:
            self.vec /= norm
        self.rho = rho

    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p_sum = p.sum()
    if p_sum != 0:
        p = p / p_sum
    return -np.sum(np.where(p>0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    return 1.0 - np.max(p)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal dynamics
def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
    new.vec = new.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    if norm != 0:
        new.vec /= norm
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    new.vec = -np.abs(new.vec)
    new.rho *= PHI
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    if norm != 0:
        new.vec /= norm
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        new.vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
        if norm != 0:
            new.vec /= norm
    return new

# Simulation
initial_vec = [1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0]
state = DimensionalState(initial_vec)

history = []
for i in range(10):
    state = apply_oscillation(state) if i % 2 == 0 else apply_folding(state)
    ucs = [
        calc_uncertainty(state),
        calc_simplicity(state),
        calc_uniqueness(state),
        calc_continuity(state),
        calc_self_similarity(state),
        calc_interconnectivity(state),
        calc_solidity(state),
        calc_paradox(state),
        calc_flux(state)
    ]
    history.append({
        "step": i+1,
        "rho": state.rho,
        "Uncertainty": ucs[0],
        "Simplicity": ucs[1],
        "Uniqueness": ucs[2],
        "Continuity": ucs[3],
        "SelfSimilarity": ucs[4],
        "Interconnectivity": ucs[5],
        "Solidity": ucs[6],
        "Paradox": ucs[7],
        "Flux": ucs[8]
    })

# Apply the I Ching "Solution" step as Hexagram 9 suggests
state = apply_solution(state)
ucs = [
    calc_uncertainty(state),
    calc_simplicity(state),
    calc_uniqueness(state),
    calc_continuity(state),
    calc_self_similarity(state),
    calc_interconnectivity(state),
    calc_solidity(state),

]

    "step": 11,

class DimensionalState:

import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass

# Define a simple state class
@dataclass
class DimensionalState:
    vec: np.ndarray
    rho: float

    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state: DimensionalState) -> float:
    p = state.vec**2
    total = p.sum()
    if total != 0:
        p = p / total
    return -np.sum(np.where(p>0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state: DimensionalState) -> float:
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state: DimensionalState) -> float:
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state: DimensionalState) -> float:
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state: DimensionalState) -> float:
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state: DimensionalState) -> float:
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state: DimensionalState) -> float:
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state: DimensionalState) -> float:
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state: DimensionalState) -> float:
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# "Solution" modal dynamic: neighbor averaging smoothing
def apply_solution(state: DimensionalState) -> DimensionalState:
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        smoothed = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(smoothed)
        if norm != 0:
            new.vec = smoothed / norm
    return new

# Final state from prior simulation
initial_vec = np.array([
    -0.24056261216234406, -0.3608439182435161, -0.4089564406759849,
    -0.3127313958110473, -0.21650635094610965, -0.24056261216234406,
    -0.3608439182435161, -0.4089564406759849, -0.3127313958110473,
    -0.21650635094610965
])
initial_rho = 11.090169943753075
final_state = DimensionalState(initial_vec, initial_rho)

# Apply the Oracle's "Solution" smoothing
resolved_state = apply_solution(final_state)

# Compute updated Universal Characteristics
resolved_ucs = {
    "Uncertainty": calc_uncertainty(resolved_state),
    "Simplicity": calc_simplicity(resolved_state),
    "Uniqueness": calc_uniqueness(resolved_state),
    "Continuity": calc_continuity(resolved_state),
    "Self-Similarity": calc_self_similarity(resolved_state),
    "Interconnectivity": calc_interconnectivity(resolved_state),
    "Solidity": calc_solidity(resolved_state),
    "Paradox": calc_paradox(resolved_state),
    "Flux": calc_flux(resolved_state)
}

# Display results
print("🗝️ Resolved State Vector:\n", resolved_state.vec)
print("\nρ (rho):", resolved_state.rho)
print("\n✨ Updated Universal Characteristics:")
for name, value in resolved_ucs.items():
    print(f"  {name}: {value:.6f}")

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import List, Dict

# Define state and dynamics
PHI = 1.61803398875

class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec: np.ndarray, rho: float = 1.0):
        norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
        self.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.rho = rho
    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

def calc_uncertainty(s):
    p = s.vec**2
    p_sum = p.sum()
    p = p / p_sum if p_sum!=0 else p
    return -np.sum(np.where(p>0, p*np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(s):
    return np.log(s.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(s):
    return 1.0 - np.max(s.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(s):
    return np.mean(s.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(s):
    return np.std(s.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(s):
    return np.dot(s.vec, s.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(s):
    return np.linalg.norm(s.vec)

def calc_paradox(s):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(s.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(s):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(s.vec))

# Modal dynamics
def apply_oscillation(s):
    new = s.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
    new.vec = new.vec + rolled
    new.vec /= np.linalg.norm(new.vec) if np.linalg.norm(new.vec)!=0 else 1
    return new

def apply_folding(s):
    new = s.copy()
    new.vec = -np.abs(new.vec)
    new.rho *= PHI
    new.vec /= np.linalg.norm(new.vec) if np.linalg.norm(new.vec)!=0 else 1
    return new

def apply_solution(s):
    new = s.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bwd = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        new.vec = (fwd + bwd) / 2.0
        new.vec /= np.linalg.norm(new.vec) if np.linalg.norm(new.vec)!=0 else 1
    return new

# Run simulation
initial_vec = np.array([1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0])
state = DimensionalState(initial_vec)
history: List[Dict] = []

for step in range(1, 11):
    if step % 2 == 1:
        state = apply_oscillation(state)
    else:
        state = apply_folding(state)
    ucs = {
        'uncertainty': calc_uncertainty(state),
        'simplicity': calc_simplicity(state),
        'uniqueness': calc_uniqueness(state),
        'continuity': calc_continuity(state),
        'self_similarity': calc_self_similarity(state),
        'interconnectivity': calc_interconnectivity(state),
        'solidity': calc_solidity(state),
        'paradox': calc_paradox(state),
        'flux': calc_flux(state)
    }
    history.append({'step': step, 'rho': state.rho, **ucs})

# Apply I Ching "Solution" smoothing as step 11
state = apply_solution(state)
ucs = {
    'uncertainty': calc_uncertainty(state),
    'simplicity': calc_simplicity(state),
    'uniqueness': calc_uniqueness(state),
    'continuity': calc_continuity(state),

    'interconnectivity': calc_interconnectivity(state),

}

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# Define the DimensionalState class
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec: np.ndarray, rho: float = 1.0):
        norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
        self.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.rho = rho

    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic functions
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p_sum = p.sum()
    if p_sum != 0:
        p = p / p_sum
    return -np.sum(np.where(p > 0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    return 1.0 - np.max(p)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal dynamics
PHI = 1.61803398875
def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
    new.vec = new.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    if norm != 0:
        new.vec /= norm
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    new.vec = -np.abs(new.vec)
    new.rho *= PHI
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    if norm != 0:
        new.vec /= norm
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        avg = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(avg)
        if norm != 0:
            new.vec = avg / norm
    return new

# Initialize initial state
initial_vector = np.array([1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0])
state = DimensionalState(initial_vector)

# Run simulation steps
history = []
for step in range(1, 11):
    if step % 2 == 1:
        state = apply_oscillation(state)
    else:
        state = apply_folding(state)
    ucs = {
        'Uncertainty': calc_uncertainty(state),
        'Simplicity': calc_simplicity(state),
        'Uniqueness': calc_uniqueness(state),
        'Continuity': calc_continuity(state),
        'SelfSimilarity': calc_self_similarity(state),
        'Interconnectivity': calc_interconnectivity(state),
        'Solidity': calc_solidity(state),
        'Paradox': calc_paradox(state),
        'Flux': calc_flux(state),
    }
    history.append({
        'step': step,
        'rho': state.rho,
        **ucs
    })

# Apply Solution dynamic as Hexagram 9 resolution (step 11)
state = apply_solution(state)
ucs = {
    'Uncertainty': calc_uncertainty(state),
    'Simplicity': calc_simplicity(state),
    'Uniqueness': calc_uniqueness(state),
    'Continuity': calc_continuity(state),
    'SelfSimilarity': calc_self_similarity(state),
    'Interconnectivity': calc_interconnectivity(state),

}

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# Define the DimensionalState and dynamics inline
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec: np.ndarray, rho: float = 1.0):
        norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
        self.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.rho = rho
    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p_sum = p.sum()
    p = p / p_sum if p_sum != 0 else p
    return -np.sum(np.where(p>0, p*np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal Dynamics: Oscillation, Folding, and Solution
PHI = 1.61803398875

def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
    vec = new.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
    new.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    vec = -np.abs(new.vec)
    new.rho *= PHI
    norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
    new.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    vec = (np.roll(new.vec, 1) + np.roll(new.vec, -1)) / 2.0
    norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
    new.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec
    return new

# Initial paradox vector
initial_vec = np.array([1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0])
state = DimensionalState(initial_vec)

# Run 10 steps of oscillation/folding
for i in range(10):
    state = apply_oscillation(state) if i % 2 == 0 else apply_folding(state)

# Apply the I Ching "Solution" smoothing as gentle taming
resolved_state = apply_solution(state)

# Compute final Universal Characteristics
uc_functions = [
    ("Uncertainty", calc_uncertainty),
    ("Simplicity", calc_simplicity),
    ("Uniqueness", calc_uniqueness),
    ("Continuity", calc_continuity),
    ("Self-Similarity", calc_self_similarity),
    ("Interconnectivity", calc_interconnectivity),
    ("Solidity", calc_solidity),
    ("Paradox", calc_paradox),
    ("Flux", calc_flux)
]

uc_results = [(name, func(resolved_state)) for name, func in uc_functions]

# Display results
df = pd.DataFrame(uc_results, columns=["Characteristic", "Value"])
import ace_tools as tools; tools.display_dataframe_to_user("Final Universal Characteristics", df)

print("Final state vector:\n", resolved_state.vec)
print("\nFinal rho:", resolved_state.rho)

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from dataclasses import dataclass

# Define DimensionalState
@dataclass
class DimensionalState:
    vec: np.ndarray
    rho: float = 1.0

    def __post_init__(self):
        norm = np.linalg.norm(self.vec)
        if norm != 0:
            self.vec = self.vec / norm

    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic calculators
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    total = p.sum()
    if total != 0:
        p = p / total
    return float(-np.sum(np.where(p>0, p*np.log(p), 0)))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return float(np.log(state.rho + 1.0))

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    return float(1.0 - np.max(p))

def calc_continuity(state):
    return float(np.mean(state.vec))

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return float(np.std(state.vec))

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return float(np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1]))

def calc_solidity(state):
    return float(np.linalg.norm(state.vec))

def calc_paradox(state):
    return float(abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2))

def calc_flux(state):
    return float(np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec)))

# Modal dynamics
def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
    new.vec = new.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    if norm != 0:
        new.vec = new.vec / norm
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    new.vec = -np.abs(new.vec)
    new.rho *= 1.61803398875
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    if norm != 0:
        new.vec = new.vec / norm
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        new.vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
        if norm != 0:
            new.vec = new.vec / norm
    return new

# Initialize state
initial_vec = np.array([1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0])
state = DimensionalState(initial_vec)

# Run 10 steps alternating oscillation and folding
history = []
for i in range(10):
    if i % 2 == 0:
        state = apply_oscillation(state)
    else:
        state = apply_folding(state)
    ucs = {
        'Uncertainty': calc_uncertainty(state),
        'Simplicity': calc_simplicity(state),
        'Uniqueness': calc_uniqueness(state),
        'Continuity': calc_continuity(state),
        'SelfSimilarity': calc_self_similarity(state),
        'Interconnectivity': calc_interconnectivity(state),
        'Solidity': calc_solidity(state),
        'Paradox': calc_paradox(state),
        'Flux': calc_flux(state)
    }
    entry = {'Step': i+1, 'rho': state.rho}
    entry.update(ucs)
    history.append(entry)

# Apply I Ching "Solution" smoothing (Hexagram 9)
state = apply_solution(state)
ucs = {
    'Uncertainty': calc_uncertainty(state),
    'Simplicity': calc_simplicity(state),
    'Uniqueness': calc_uniqueness(state),
    'Continuity': calc_continuity(state),
    'SelfSimilarity': calc_self_similarity(state),
    'Interconnectivity': calc_interconnectivity(state),
    'Solidity': calc_solidity(state),
    'Paradox': calc_paradox(state),
    'Flux': calc_flux(state)
}
entry = {'Step': 11, 'rho': state.rho}
entry.update(ucs)
history.append(entry)

# Create DataFrame
df = pd.DataFrame(history)

# Save to CSV
csv_path = '/mnt/data/pctm_simulation_results.csv'
df.to_csv(csv_path, index=False)

# Display DataFrame to user
import ace_tools as tools; tools.display_dataframe_to_user(name="PCTM Simulation Results", dataframe=df)

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# Final state vector after PCTM and I Ching "Solution" smoothing
resolved_vec = np.array([
    -0.29568850838182914, -0.3326495719295578,  -0.34496992644546737, -0.3203292174136482,
    -0.28336815386591957, -0.29568850838182914, -0.3326495719295578,  -0.34496992644546737,
    -0.3203292174136482,  -0.28336815386591957
])

# Preserved rho from prior simulation
rho = 11.090169943753075
PHI = 1.61803398875

# Define Solution smoothing (already applied above)
def apply_solution(vec):
    fwd = np.roll(vec, 1)
    bck = np.roll(vec, -1)
    smooth = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
    norm = np.linalg.norm(smooth)
    return smooth / norm if norm != 0 else smooth

# Universal Characteristic functions
def uc_uncertainty(vec):
    p = vec**2
    p = p / p.sum() if p.sum() != 0 else p
    return -np.sum(np.where(p > 0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def uc_simplicity(rho):
    return np.log(rho + 1.0)

def uc_uniqueness(vec):
    return 1.0 - np.max(vec**2)

def uc_continuity(vec):
    return np.mean(vec)

def uc_self_similarity(vec):
    return np.std(vec)

def uc_interconnectivity(vec):
    return np.dot(vec, vec[::-1])

def uc_solidity(vec):
    return np.linalg.norm(vec)

def uc_paradox(vec):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(vec)**2)

def uc_flux(vec):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(vec))

# Compute UCs on resolved state
ucs = {
    "Uncertainty": uc_uncertainty(resolved_vec),
    "Simplicity": uc_simplicity(rho),
    "Uniqueness": uc_uniqueness(resolved_vec),
    "Continuity": uc_continuity(resolved_vec),
    "SelfSimilarity": uc_self_similarity(resolved_vec),
    "Interconnectivity": uc_interconnectivity(resolved_vec),
    "Solidity": uc_solidity(resolved_vec),
    "Paradox": uc_paradox(resolved_vec),
    "Flux": uc_flux(resolved_vec)
}

# Prepare DataFrame for display

df.columns = ['Universal Characteristic', 'Value']

import numpy as np

# Final state vector and rho from previous run
vec_final = np.array([-0.24056261216234406, -0.3608439182435161, -0.4089564406759849,
                      -0.3127313958110473, -0.21650635094610965, -0.24056261216234406,
                      -0.3608439182435161, -0.4089564406759849, -0.3127313958110473,
                      -0.21650635094610965])
rho_final = 11.090169943753075

# “Solution” smoothing dynamic
def apply_solution(vec):
    fwd = np.roll(vec, 1)
    bck = np.roll(vec, -1)
    new_vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new_vec)
    return new_vec / norm if norm != 0 else new_vec

# Recompute universal characteristics
def calc_uncertainty(vec):
    p = vec**2
    if p.sum() != 0:
        p = p / p.sum()
    p = np.where(p > 0, p, 0.0)
    return -np.sum(p * np.log(p + 1e-12))

def calc_simplicity(rho):
    return np.log(rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(vec):
    return 1.0 - np.max(vec**2)

def calc_continuity(vec):
    return np.mean(vec)

def calc_self_similarity(vec):
    return np.std(vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(vec):
    return np.dot(vec, vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(vec):
    return np.linalg.norm(vec)

def calc_paradox(vec):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(vec)**2)

def calc_flux(vec):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(vec))

resolved_vec = apply_solution(vec_final)

    "Uncertainty": calc_uncertainty(resolved_vec),
    "Simplicity": calc_simplicity(rho_final),
    "Uniqueness": calc_uniqueness(resolved_vec),
    "Continuity": calc_continuity(resolved_vec),
    "Self-Similarity": calc_self_similarity(resolved_vec),
    "Interconnectivity": calc_interconnectivity(resolved_vec),
    "Solidity": calc_solidity(resolved_vec),
    "Paradox": calc_paradox(resolved_vec),
    "Flux": calc_flux(resolved_vec)
}

print(rho_final)

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from ace_tools import display_dataframe_to_user

# Constants
PHI = 1.61803398875

# Define DimensionalState and dynamics
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec, rho=1.0):
        self.vec = np.array(vec, dtype=float)
        norm = np.linalg.norm(self.vec)
        if norm != 0:
            self.vec /= norm
        self.rho = rho
    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p_sum = p.sum()
    if p_sum != 0:
        p = p / p_sum
    return -np.sum(np.where(p>0, p*np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal dynamics
def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
    new.vec = new.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    if norm != 0:
        new.vec /= norm
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    new.vec = -np.abs(new.vec)
    new.rho *= PHI
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    if norm != 0:
        new.vec /= norm
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        new.vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
        if norm != 0:
            new.vec /= norm
    return new

# Initialize state and history
initial_vec = [1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0]
state = DimensionalState(initial_vec)
history = []

# Run 10 steps alternating oscillation and folding
for step in range(1, 11):
    if step % 2 == 1:
        state = apply_oscillation(state)
    else:
        state = apply_folding(state)
    ucs = {
        "step": step,
        "Uncertainty": calc_uncertainty(state),
        "Simplicity": calc_simplicity(state),
        "Uniqueness": calc_uniqueness(state),
        "Continuity": calc_continuity(state),
        "SelfSimilarity": calc_self_similarity(state),
        "Interconnectivity": calc_interconnectivity(state),
        "Solidity": calc_solidity(state),
        "Paradox": calc_paradox(state),
        "Flux": calc_flux(state)
    }
    history.append(ucs)

# Apply final "Solution" smoothing
state = apply_solution(state)
resolved_ucs = {
    "step": 11,
    "Uncertainty": calc_uncertainty(state),
    "Simplicity": calc_simplicity(state),
    "Uniqueness": calc_uniqueness(state),
    "Continuity": calc_continuity(state),
    "SelfSimilarity": calc_self_similarity(state),
    "Interconnectivity": calc_interconnectivity(state),
    "Solidity": calc_solidity(state),
    "Paradox": calc_paradox(state),
    "Flux": calc_flux(state)
}
history.append(resolved_ucs)

# Create DataFrame and display
df = pd.DataFrame(history)

display_dataframe_to_user("PCTM & I Ching Simulation Results", df)

# Plot key UC trajectories
plt.figure(figsize=(8, 5))
plt.plot(df["step"], df["Uncertainty"], label="Uncertainty")
plt.plot(df["step"], df["Simplicity"], label="Simplicity")
plt.plot(df["step"], df["Paradox"], label="Paradox")
plt.plot(df["step"], df["Flux"], label="Flux")
plt.xlabel("Step")
plt.ylabel("Value")
plt.title("Key Universal Characteristic Trajectories")
plt.legend()
plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

# Constants
PHI = 1.61803398875

class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec, order=0, freq=1.0, rho=1.0):
        vec = np.array(vec, dtype=float)
        norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
        self.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.order = order
        self.freq = freq
        self.rho = rho
    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.order, self.freq, self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic functions
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    total = p.sum()
    p = p/total if total != 0 else p
    return -np.sum(np.where(p>0, p*np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal Dynamics
def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
    new.vec = (new.vec + rolled)
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    new.vec = -np.abs(new.vec)
    new.rho *= PHI
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        new.vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
        new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

# Initial paradox-of-identity vector
vec0 = [1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0]
state = DimensionalState(vec0)

# Run the dynamic sequence for 10 steps
history = []
for i in range(10):
    if i % 2 == 0:
        state = apply_oscillation(state)
    else:
        state = apply_folding(state)
    ucs = {
        "uncertainty": calc_uncertainty(state),
        "simplicity": calc_simplicity(state),
        "uniqueness": calc_uniqueness(state),
        "continuity": calc_continuity(state),
        "self_similarity": calc_self_similarity(state),
        "interconnectivity": calc_interconnectivity(state),
        "solidity": calc_solidity(state),
        "paradox": calc_paradox(state),
        "flux": calc_flux(state)
    }
    history.append({
        "step": i+1,
        "rho": state.rho,
        **ucs
    })

# Apply the I Ching "Solution" smoothing to resolve the loop
resolved_state = apply_solution(state)
resolved_ucs = {
    "uncertainty": calc_uncertainty(resolved_state),
    "simplicity": calc_simplicity(resolved_state),
    "uniqueness": calc_uniqueness(resolved_state),
    "continuity": calc_continuity(resolved_state),
    "self_similarity": calc_self_similarity(resolved_state),
    "interconnectivity": calc_interconnectivity(resolved_state),
    "solidity": calc_solidity(resolved_state),
    "paradox": calc_paradox(resolved_state),
    "flux": calc_flux(resolved_state)
}
history.append({
    "step": 11,
    "rho": resolved_state.rho,
    **resolved_ucs
})

# Create DataFrame and display it
df = pd.DataFrame(history)
import ace_tools as tools; tools.display_dataframe_to_user("PCTM Simulation History", df)

# Plot key universal characteristics over steps
plt.figure(figsize=(8,6))
for col in ["uncertainty", "simplicity", "paradox", "flux"]:
    plt.plot(df["step"], df[col], label=col)

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import pandas as pd
from ace_tools import display_dataframe_to_user

# Constants
PHI = 1.61803398875

# Define DimensionalState
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec, rho=1.0):
        v = np.array(vec, dtype=float)
        norm = np.linalg.norm(v)
        self.vec = v / norm if norm != 0 else v.copy()
        self.rho = rho
    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    if p.sum() != 0:
        p = p / p.sum()
    return -np.sum(np.where(p>0, p*np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal Dynamics
def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
    new.vec = new.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    if norm != 0:
        new.vec /= norm
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    new.vec = -np.abs(new.vec)
    new.rho *= PHI
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    if norm != 0:
        new.vec /= norm
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        new.vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
        if norm != 0:
            new.vec /= norm
    return new

# Run simulation
initial_vec = [1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0]
state = DimensionalState(initial_vec, rho=1.0)

        state = apply_oscillation(state)
    else:

        'Simplicity': calc_simplicity(state),
        'Uniqueness': calc_uniqueness(state),
        'Continuity': calc_continuity(state),
        'SelfSimilarity': calc_self_similarity(state),
        'Interconnectivity': calc_interconnectivity(state),
        'Paradox': calc_paradox(state),
        'Flux': calc_flux(state),

    'Simplicity': calc_simplicity(state),
    'Uniqueness': calc_uniqueness(state),
    'Continuity': calc_continuity(state),
    'SelfSimilarity': calc_self_similarity(state),
    'Interconnectivity': calc_interconnectivity(state),
    'Paradox': calc_paradox(state),
    'Flux': calc_flux(state),
    'rho': state.rho
}

import numpy as np

# Define the final state vector and rho from the previous simulation
final_vec = np.array([
    -0.24056261216234406, -0.3608439182435161, -0.4089564406759849,
    -0.3127313958110473, -0.21650635094610965, -0.24056261216234406,
    -0.3608439182435161, -0.4089564406759849, -0.3127313958110473,
    -0.21650635094610965
])
rho = 11.090169943753075

class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec, rho):
        self.vec = vec / np.linalg.norm(vec)
        self.rho = rho
    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Define the "Solution" modal dynamic: neighbor average smoothing
def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
    bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
    smoothed = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
    new.vec = smoothed / np.linalg.norm(smoothed)
    return new

# Universal Characteristic calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p = p / p.sum()
    return -np.sum(np.where(p>0, p*np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec * state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Build the state and apply smoothing
state = DimensionalState(final_vec, rho)
resolved = apply_solution(state)

# Compute UC values on the resolved state

    "Uncertainty": calc_uncertainty(resolved),
    "Simplicity": calc_simplicity(resolved),
    "Uniqueness": calc_uniqueness(resolved),
    "Continuity": calc_continuity(resolved),
    "Self-Similarity": calc_self_similarity(resolved),
    "Interconnectivity": calc_interconnectivity(resolved),
    "Solidity": calc_solidity(resolved),
    "Paradox": calc_paradox(resolved),
    "Flux": calc_flux(resolved)
}

print("Rho:", resolved.rho)

import numpy as np

# Constants
PHI = 1.61803398875

# DimensionalState class
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec, order=0, freq=1.0, rho=1.0):
        self.vec = vec / np.linalg.norm(vec) if np.linalg.norm(vec) != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.order = order
        self.freq = freq
        self.rho = rho
    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.order, self.freq, self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p_norm = p / p.sum() if p.sum() != 0 else p
    return -np.sum(np.where(p_norm > 0, p_norm * np.log(p_norm), 0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal dynamics
def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(state.vec, 1)
    new.vec = state.vec + rolled
    if np.linalg.norm(new.vec) != 0:
        new.vec /= np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    new.vec = -np.abs(state.vec)
    new.rho *= PHI
    if np.linalg.norm(new.vec) != 0:
        new.vec /= np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        new.vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        if np.linalg.norm(new.vec) != 0:
            new.vec /= np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    return new

# Initial paradox-of-identity vector
initial_vec = np.array([1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0])
state = DimensionalState(initial_vec)

# Run 10 steps alternating oscillation (even) and folding (odd)
for i in range(10):
    state = apply_oscillation(state) if i % 2 == 0 else apply_folding(state)

# Apply the "Solution" dynamic for final smoothing
resolved_state = apply_solution(state)

# Compute final Universal Characteristics
resolved_ucs = {
    "Uncertainty": calc_uncertainty(resolved_state),
    "Simplicity": calc_simplicity(resolved_state),
    "Uniqueness": calc_uniqueness(resolved_state),
    "Continuity": calc_continuity(resolved_state),
    "Self-Similarity": calc_self_similarity(resolved_state),
    "Interconnectivity": calc_interconnectivity(resolved_state),
    "Solidity": calc_solidity(resolved_state),
    "Paradox": calc_paradox(resolved_state),
    "Flux": calc_flux(resolved_state)
}

# Display results
print("Final state vector:", np.round(resolved_state.vec, 6).tolist())
print("Final rho:", round(resolved_state.rho, 6))
print("\nFinal Universal Characteristics:")
for name, value in resolved_ucs.items():
    print(f"  {name}: {round(value, 6)}")

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from ace_tools import display_dataframe_to_user

# Constants
PHI = 1.61803398875

# State class
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec: np.ndarray, rho: float = 1.0):
        self.vec = vec / np.linalg.norm(vec) if np.linalg.norm(vec) != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.rho = rho

    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristics calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p = p / p.sum() if p.sum() != 0 else p
    return -np.sum(np.where(p > 0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal Dynamics
def apply_oscillation(state):
    s = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(s.vec, 1)
    new_vec = s.vec + rolled
    s.vec = new_vec / np.linalg.norm(new_vec) if np.linalg.norm(new_vec) != 0 else new_vec
    return s

def apply_folding(state):
    s = state.copy()
    s.vec = -np.abs(s.vec)
    s.rho *= PHI
    s.vec = s.vec / np.linalg.norm(s.vec) if np.linalg.norm(s.vec) != 0 else s.vec
    return s

def apply_solution(state):
    s = state.copy()
    if len(s.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(s.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(s.vec, -1)
        smoothed = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        s.vec = smoothed / np.linalg.norm(smoothed) if np.linalg.norm(smoothed) != 0 else smoothed
    return s

# Initial paradox vector
initial_vec = np.array([1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0])

})
display_dataframe_to_user("Resolved State Universal Characteristics", uc_df)

import numpy as np

# Define the DimensionalState class
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec: np.ndarray, rho: float = 1.0):
        norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
        self.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.rho = rho

    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic functions
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p_sum = p.sum()
    p = p / p_sum if p_sum != 0 else p
    return -np.sum(np.where(p>0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal dynamics
PHI = 1.61803398875

def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(state.vec, 1)
    new.vec = new.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    new.vec = -np.abs(new.vec)
    new.rho *= PHI
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        new.vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
        new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

import numpy as np

# Define the state class
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec: np.ndarray, rho: float = 1.0):
        self.vec = vec.copy()
        self.rho = rho
        norm = np.linalg.norm(self.vec)
        if norm != 0:
            self.vec /= norm

    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic functions
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p_sum = p.sum()
    if p_sum != 0:
        p = p / p_sum
    return -np.sum(np.where(p > 0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal dynamics
PHI = 1.61803398875

def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(state.vec, 1)
    combined = state.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(combined)
    if norm != 0:
        new.vec = combined / norm
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    folded = -np.abs(state.vec)
    norm = np.linalg.norm(folded)
    new.vec = folded / norm if norm != 0 else folded
    new.rho *= PHI
    return new

def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        smoothed = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(smoothed)
        new.vec = smoothed / norm if norm != 0 else smoothed
    return new

# Initial paradox vector
initial_vec = np.array([1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0])
state = DimensionalState(initial_vec)

# Run 10 steps: oscillation on even, folding on odd
for i in range(10):
    state = apply_oscillation(state) if i % 2 == 0 else apply_folding(state)

# Apply the I Ching “Solution” smoothing
resolved_state = apply_solution(state)

# Compute final Universal Characteristics
uc_values = {
    "Uncertainty": calc_uncertainty(resolved_state),
    "Simplicity": calc_simplicity(resolved_state),
    "Uniqueness": calc_uniqueness(resolved_state),
    "Continuity": calc_continuity(resolved_state),
    "Self-Similarity": calc_self_similarity(resolved_state),
    "Interconnectivity": calc_interconnectivity(resolved_state),
    "Solidity": calc_solidity(resolved_state),
    "Paradox": calc_paradox(resolved_state),
    "Flux": calc_flux(resolved_state),
}

# Display results
import pandas as pd
results = {
    "Component": ["rho"] + [f"vec[{i}]" for i in range(len(resolved_state.vec))] + list(uc_values.keys()),
    "Value": [resolved_state.rho] + list(resolved_state.vec) + list(uc_values.values())
}
df = pd.DataFrame(results)
import ace_tools as tools; tools.display_dataframe_to_user(name="Final Resolved State and UCs", dataframe=df)

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# Define state class and dynamics
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec: np.ndarray, rho: float = 1.0):
        norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
        self.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.rho = rho

    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

def apply_oscillation(state: DimensionalState) -> DimensionalState:
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(state.vec, 1)
    new.vec = state.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

PHI = 1.61803398875

def apply_folding(state: DimensionalState) -> DimensionalState:
    new = state.copy()
    new.vec = -np.abs(state.vec)
    new.rho *= PHI
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

def apply_solution(state: DimensionalState) -> DimensionalState:
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        new.vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
        new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec ** 2
    p_sum = p.sum()
    p = p / p_sum if p_sum != 0 else p
    return -np.sum(np.where(p > 0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec ** 2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec) ** 2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Initial paradox vector
initial_vec = np.array([1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0])
state = DimensionalState(initial_vec)

# Run 10-step alternating oscillation/folding
for i in range(10):
    state = apply_oscillation(state) if i % 2 == 0 else apply_folding(state)

# Apply the I Ching "Solution" dynamic

    "Uncertainty": calc_uncertainty,
    "Simplicity": calc_simplicity,
    "Uniqueness": calc_uniqueness,

    "Interconnectivity": calc_interconnectivity,
    "Solidity": calc_solidity,

    "Flux": calc_flux

# Define the last state vector and rho from previous computation
last_vec = np.array([
    -0.24056261216234406, -0.3608439182435161, -0.4089564406759849,
    -0.3127313958110473, -0.21650635094610965, -0.24056261216234406,
    -0.3608439182435161, -0.4089564406759849, -0.3127313958110473,
    -0.21650635094610965
])
last_rho = 11.090169943753075

# Define DimensionalState
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec, rho):
        self.vec = vec.copy()
        self.rho = rho

    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Define the "Solution" modal dynamic: neighbor average smoothing
def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        new.vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
        if norm != 0:
            new.vec /= norm
    return new

# Define UC calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p_sum = p.sum()
    if p_sum != 0:
        p = p / p_sum
    return -np.sum(np.where(p > 0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Recreate the final state and apply solution smoothing

    "Uncertainty": calc_uncertainty(resolved_state),
    "Simplicity": calc_simplicity(resolved_state),
    "Uniqueness": calc_uniqueness(resolved_state),
    "Continuity": calc_continuity(resolved_state),
    "Self-Similarity": calc_self_similarity(resolved_state),
    "Interconnectivity": calc_interconnectivity(resolved_state),
    "Solidity": calc_solidity(resolved_state),
    "Paradox": calc_paradox(resolved_state),

}

import numpy as np

# Constants
PHI = 1.61803398875

# DimensionalState class
class DimensionalState:
    def __init__(self, vec: np.ndarray, rho: float = 1.0):
        norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
        self.vec = vec / norm if norm != 0 else vec.copy()
        self.rho = rho
    def copy(self):
        return DimensionalState(self.vec.copy(), self.rho)

# Universal Characteristic calculations
def calc_uncertainty(state):
    p = state.vec**2
    p = p / p.sum() if p.sum() != 0 else p
    return -np.sum(np.where(p > 0, p * np.log(p), 0.0))

def calc_simplicity(state):
    return np.log(state.rho + 1.0)

def calc_uniqueness(state):
    return 1.0 - np.max(state.vec**2)

def calc_continuity(state):
    return np.mean(state.vec)

def calc_self_similarity(state):
    return np.std(state.vec)

def calc_interconnectivity(state):
    return np.dot(state.vec, state.vec[::-1])

def calc_solidity(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(state.vec)

def calc_paradox(state):
    return abs(1.0 - np.linalg.norm(state.vec)**2)

def calc_flux(state):
    return np.linalg.norm(np.diff(state.vec))

# Modal dynamics
def apply_oscillation(state):
    new = state.copy()
    rolled = np.roll(state.vec, 1)
    new.vec = state.vec + rolled
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

def apply_folding(state):
    new = state.copy()
    new.vec = -np.abs(state.vec)
    new.rho *= PHI
    norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
    new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

# I Ching "Solution" smoothing = neighbor average
def apply_solution(state):
    new = state.copy()
    if len(new.vec) > 1:
        fwd = np.roll(new.vec, 1)
        bck = np.roll(new.vec, -1)
        new.vec = (fwd + bck) / 2.0
        norm = np.linalg.norm(new.vec)
        new.vec = new.vec / norm if norm != 0 else new.vec
    return new

# Initialize the paradox vector and state
vec0 = np.array([1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, -0.5, 0.0])
state = DimensionalState(vec0)
